149. **THE LAST THREE DECISIONS ANSWERED AND PROPAGATED — D15, D21, D37; `DECISIONS_PENDING.md`'s open set is EMPTY for the first time (2026-08-26, Opus 5 executor, task F37, dispatched by Claude Fable 5; record-only — no code, no data, no harness behaviour; two read-only probes).** Answers: D15 [vixy 2026-08-26, harness `2b24a1b`], D21 + D37 [vixy 2026-08-22, harness `d521807a`]. **D15's four sub-decisions land as four different outcomes**: (a) VIEW_HORIZON **DISSOLVES** — *"the new system avoid the use of up vector … so the VIEW_HORIZON is no longer usefull"* ⇒ no new-path mechanism owed, but this is NOT authority to retire the config value (veto); (b) freeMode swapping *"must be transparent … in both ways"* is a REQUIREMENT that judges §11.112's three options rather than choosing among them (auto-disable excluded, resume-from-present excluded, restore-with-warp excluded ⇒ a fourth shape: suspend + minimum-acceleration heading recovery); (c) *"continual tracking must be preserved and smooth"* ratifies mirror-all-four and adds a SERVO acceptance criterion (telescope analogy); (d) **OVERRIDES the standing rec** (*"no key"*) — init/reinit initializes the state *"which now include the freeMode"*, `config.ini` configures it, default `attached=True`, with a **DELEGATED VETO** on the name (*"you can veto if the inferred meaning contrast with the observed ones"*). All four carry Vixy's own INFORM-the-tester clause ⇒ a reopening trigger, §11.113(i) shape. **D15(b) ⇒ §5.80's converter fix reads as AUTHORIZED** [derived, veto stated]: the swap's largest observable is an 11 300 km / 124.6797° teleport at 6354 px>8 against a 0 px A/A floor, and a perceptual transparency criterion cannot exclude it; the two §11.144 riders (`moveto lat/lon` meaning, `get status position`) are NOT decided. **D21** = §2(a)'s two-layer rule in Vixy's words — *"Unscaled for physics (orbit, shadow received and casted), scaled for bounding/rendering, grounded children inherit scaling (to be visually identical to unscaled … the referential is the body surface)"*: the load-time `altStart` latch dies by implication (a baked constant cannot inherit a 5 s ramp), *"visually identical to unscaled"* is an ACCEPTANCE CRITERION = uniform dilation of the surface neighbourhood (placement AND extent; rival radial-only reading is the veto point), and the REACH half of `boundingRadius` (§11.96(e)) is explicitly NOT decided by *"scaled for bounding"*. **D37 is DELEGATED to the tester — and its premise was PROBED, which is this entry's one measurement.** Vixy: *"every bodies have the star as parent body, in which case hiding the star hide every bodies in the system."* **The premise is CONFIRMED and the datum that appeared to contradict it is REFUTED**: `~/.spacecrafter/ssystem.ini` (md5 `545a51ef`) has 90 body sections and **exactly one `parent = none` — `[sun]`**; the runtime tree reads `Moon → Earth → Sun → SolarSystem → MilkyWay → Universe` with **91 of 120 bodies strictly under Sun** and all **28** that are not carrying `boundingRadius = 0` + `visible: false` (system nodes and hidden anchors); and on `b39_star`'s own committed artifacts the Moon with the Sun hidden is **not dimmer but GONE** — disc-interior mean **4.738 → 0.000**, **95.0 %** of the 1217 px>32 inside 150 px of the Moon's centre, stars visible through the former disc, both entries pixel-identical. **§11.117(k)(1)'s *"the Moon stays lit … the 1217 px difference is the Sun's own disc and halo leaving"* is CORRECTED** (the Sun reads `visible: false` in BOTH frames): the counts stand, the reading does not. Mechanism = `b39_star.py`'s `lit()` counts the whole 2048² frame, star-field-dominated (15 462 px background vs ~1 850 px Moon), so total disappearance moves it 11 % — the criterion-that-cannot-discriminate class → **NEW §5.99**. Consequence carried to the tester WITH the question: **on shipped content D37's two options produce the SAME frame**; there is no second route (a descendant cannot be un-hidden under a hidden ancestor); the question is reachable only in an AUTHORED scene (a body `parent = none` beside the star) or a multi-star delivery; and today's behaviour is option (1) as a SOURCE fact (`ModularSystem.cpp:239-252`, unconditional `updateAsLightSource`). Second probe: **`doc/superscript.sts` was rewritten by the tester at code HEAD `f0c8ef83`** (+267/−67) and **11 of 13 script-side witnesses are gone** — the 0xA0 fixed, `duration 5` dropped, `date_display_*` and `zrot/yrot` RESPELLED, `movetocity`/`set mode`/`marseille`/`wait action reset_timer`/`image … spacecraft` removed, indented `#` gone; **SURVIVING: `landscape … spacecraft on` byte-identical (SS-9) and the `$body_selected` table (§5.98/SS-17)**; SS-16 answered BY ACTION (fixed in place) ⇒ the historical witness moves to `70dee810:doc/superscript.sts`, now a load-bearing pointer; the +267 added lines are UNEXAMINED and the scedit corpus gate is not re-runnable in this tree. Six final-pass members added (D37 question + premise fact; D15(a)–(d) INFORM ×4; D15(b) heading CONFIRM). Three `vixy-side-ideas.txt` sketches indexed as POINTER entries in FEATURE_REQUESTS (parallel-script · script-binding · script-trigger; the txt stays the text authority). Rows: **B18** resolved per D15 ((c)+(d) ⇒ F38), **§5.27** behaviour half UNBLOCKED (⇒ F39), **B31** T3 unblocked, **B35** + **§5.89** annotated INFORMED-not-decided, **B39** residue → final tester pass. **One AUTHORITY INVERSION found while flipping §5.27 (§5.2 class):** the expanded entry `INTENT/5.27.md` — the authority by the header's own rule — still carried the root §11.101(f) REFUTED a month ago, while the correction had been written only onto the derived INTENT.md stub; repaired in place, but nothing checks that a stub correction reaches its entry file and the 2026-07-23 split created one such pair per expanded entry ⇒ **an owed cross-check sweep, named not run.** […] → INTENT/11.149.md

150. **D15(c)+(d) IMPLEMENTED — the sky lock's four bypass sites become BOTH-PATHS, and `Core::init` stops force-resetting camera state it should be reading from config (2026-08-26, Opus 5 executor, task F38, dispatched by Claude Fable 5; code `f0c8ef83 → b444d381`).** Acceptance is SERVO-grade per §11.149(a3) — *the drawn view behaves like old's across the transition*, measured at the view layer and on the composed screen, both ways, against the pre-change binary on the SAME instrument (the dual dump already carried both paths' flags and view matrices, so no instrument code was written). **Census adjudicated**: §11.112's `971/2066/1251/1289` and the dispatch's `1083/1372/1410/2313` are the SAME four call sites — pure line drift, each context re-read at `606d6b87`; nothing joined the set, nothing left it. The dispatch's *"the zoom-out family now routes through the mirror"* is corrected — `autoZoomOut` poked directly until this entry; it is the **turn/drag** family that always mirrored. **PRE-change, all four DESYNC and live on the shipped surface**: `flag lock_sky_position on` → `zoom auto initial` left old released (**18.0493°** over 0.05 d) while the drawn path kept holding (**0.0000°**), **131 089 px>32** against a **0 px** A/A floor; select-while-tracking was the mirror image (old 0.0000° held, new 18.0493° drifting, 3 287 px). **POST-change ALL PASS both ways, both rounds of both reversible scenes** (released 18.0493/18.0493; held 0.0000/0.0081–0.0167). **One coupling was forced and is named**: at site 1083 the lock mirror alone is INERT, because the Camera's sky-lock is dormant while `target` is set and the companion `setFlagTraking(0)` was old-only — measured pre-change as `camera.tracked='Mars'` beside `old flagTraking=0`. So the tracking clear at the three lock sites that carry one now routes through `Core::setFlagTracking`; **no tracking write outside the four mandated sites was touched**. **Old-path bit-identity asserted by measurement**: 32 matched samples, every old flag equal, heading/viewOffset exactly 0 apart, continuous observables ≤ **2.72e-05°** — predicted by the runs' **8.1e-08 d** jd offset (2.9e-05°). **(d)**: two `[navigation]` keys, `attached = true` (freeMode = !attached) and `flag_lock_sky_position = false`, both defaulting to today's behaviour so no default acts (D12); read `findEntry`-first because `InitParser::getBoolean` returns FALSE for a missing key and every fielded config lacks them — a naive read would have started every existing install in free flight. Verified on a temp-HOME farm (field config `03fbee59` pristine in and out of all four legs): no key ⇒ freeMode/skyLocked false, no log, no parser warning (positive control on the same channel fires); `attached=false` + lock true ⇒ both act, both LOGGED with the way back, free position **4.2635736e-05 AU** = the same place the anchored distance names. **NAME VETO: `attached` ADOPTED** — it describes `freeMode == false` exactly, so the veto condition is not met; the real hazard, recorded at the define, is `Camera::boundToSurface`, a SECOND attachment *"attached"* also reads onto ⇒ an ambiguity, not a contradiction, and whoever gives that member a key may not call it `attached`. **D13 answered by measurement, both routes**: version equal ⇒ an unknown key survives byte-identical and unread (*"config.ini is up to date"*); version different ⇒ `checkUselessKey` removes it with a warning and rewrites the file, in the same run that inserted the two new keys at their defaults. Downgrade tolerated, never fatal, with settings lost on a version bump. Also corrected: `config.ini` is **not** rewritten at shutdown — only by the TUI/`configuration action save` and by CheckConfig on a version mismatch. **Residual characterized, not tolerated**: the held pairs read 0.0081–0.0167° against old's exact 0, and `lockedSkyRot` is identical at both samples (0.000000°) — the hold TARGET never moves; the error is the per-frame float32 ZXZ round-trip (`recoverParams`→`viewRotation`), measured 0.000000–0.012680° per frame, pre-existing and untouched (no Camera code in the diff), **0.095 px** at the shipped 2048/180° fisheye ⇒ invisible; §11.58's *"0.0000°"* is true of its own configuration (the degenerate extraction axis at the init view), not of the hold in general. B18 re-run green and reproducing §11.58's numbers; its probe-count expectation superseded in place (init now calls the mirror, and init runs twice ⇒ 6 entries, not 4). **A–D battery run PRE and POST on one config** (it passes through site 2313, so a single post run could only have said *"still runs"*): the ONLY non-numeric difference across all five dumps, 153 records each, is `camera.skyLocked` **False → True in scenes C/D/D′** — where the old path's flag was **already 1** — with `heading` 0→0, both old flags unchanged, and body ephemeris agreeing to **≤3.7e-09 AU**; the fix reproduced on scenes written a month before it. The pre-change binary rebuilt from `f0c8ef83` came back **md5-identical (`f1c47308`) to the 2026-08-23 binary**, which retroactively certifies the (b) baseline. Running it found **NEW §5.102** (the two display-scaled bodies, `Moon` and `Sun`, dump `boundingRadius`/`scaledDatumRadius`/`scaledGroundRadius` = **nan** while `visible: true`; the common factor is the `ASmooth scaling` member, §11.16's own 0/0 class, and it reaches the OBSERVER — `getAltitudeReference()` returns `scaledDatumRadius`, so on the Moon `Camera::moveTo` writes `distance = nan` and the drawn path's altitude readout answers **nan** beside old's 100 m; 73 of 153 records affected — **D21/F39's own subject**) and **NEW §5.103** (the dump prints bare `nan`, which is not JSON, so `analyze.py` cannot read a scene-C dump at all and dies on 2 body lines in every other scene; the old path's half of the same line already solves this with `jnum()`), both identical in the two legs ⇒ pre-existing. **NEW §5.100** (`zoom auto in` starts OLD tracking only: `camera.tracked` stays empty while old centres the body at (1024,1024), and the fov mirror zooms the drawn path to a **0.0023°** field pointing away from it — D15(c)'s own use case through another command) and **NEW §5.101** (`autoZoomOut` re-aims only the OLD view: 99.0318° vs 0.0214°, the two paths end **99° apart**) — measured, recorded, NOT fixed. […] → INTENT/11.150.md
151. **TWO MID-ROUND RATIFICATIONS [vixy 2026-08-26] — §11.149's (b) and (c3) vetoes DISSOLVE (Claude Fable 5, supervising; record-only).** (a) D15(b) transparency INCLUDES the position channel (*"otherwise it's no longer transparent"*) ⇒ **§5.80's converter-half fix is decision-free** (= task F40); riders stay open; the uniform swap principle recorded — everything that CAN stay continuous DOES (bodies: D28's landed hold; mode: position + orientation). (b) D21's criterion RATIFIED as **uniform dilation** (*"Yes, uniform dilation is required"*) ⇒ F39 gates on placement AND extent. […] → INTENT/11.151.md

152. **D21 IMPLEMENTED — display scaling becomes a PRESENTATION transform, grounded children inherit it as a UNIFORM DILATION, the model layer keeps the truth; and its precondition §5.102 is ROOTED at an indeterminate phase timer that had been there since `ASmooth` was written (2026-08-26, Opus 5 executor, task F39, dispatched by Claude Fable 5; code `b444d381 → 7ef11aca`, submodule `224eba7 → 7ce5835`).** Everything measured on the SHIPPED config (`moon_scale = 5`, `flag_moon_scaled = true`), **no `flag moon_scaled off` anywhere** — the §11.78(a) mandate scene at last in the configuration a planetarium ships. **§5.102's root**: `ASmooth::set`'s fresh-movement branch wrote every coefficient except `timer`, and the constructor wrote none [observed: `ASmooth.hpp:55-60`, `:11-13`]; measured by in-binary read-back, the Moon's `scaling` returns `-nan` **immediately after its first `setScaling`**, before any retarget — which also DEFEATS §11.16's `b == 0` guard (`b += timer*a*2` is NaN, never 0), so that characterization is narrowed, not refuted. **Date discrimination: neither config-dependent nor a regression — the defect is ORIGINAL** (`ca3205f`, the commit that added the file) and what varies is the GARBAGE: two runs of one binary read `a = -2.18e-39` vs `1.12e-08` where only ctor-written `c` is stable, and §11.150's own artifacts hold all three outcomes on the SAME binary and config (`nan`, stuck-at-×1 `1.16138e-05`, correct ×5 `5.80690103e-05`) — exactly what an undefined phase time predicts. Fix at the root (`timer = 0`, forced by the same `p(t)` the coefficients solve, plus class-wide default initializers); it is load-bearing beyond the first movement, since `update()` leaves a completed phase's timer in place. Post-fix the A–D battery carries **zero non-finite values** (pre: 73/153 records). **D21**: the `SurfacePointOrbit` load-time latch is dead — the parent is held through a `ModularBodyPtr` and the RAW datum read at every evaluation (4th instance of the B15/B19/B32 class; closes §11.101(f)(iii)'s D8 leak; makes the runtime `datum_radius` seam reach grounded children); the presentation half is `inheritedScaling`, **pushed by the parent from `updateCache`** (I3), which is what makes the 5 s ramp live, with `getDisplayScaling()` (extent) and `getDisplayEclipticPos()` (placement) as the two seams. **The gate is one scalar and it is the ratified criterion**: rover extent over the Moon's DISPLAYED datum — PRE **0.27716** scaled vs 1.38582 unscaled (the rival reading §11.151(b) killed, measured) and drifting mid-ramp; POST **1.3858165 ± 1.5e-7 across all thirteen legs**. `|eclDisplay| = 8687.000 km` == Moon `scaledDatumRadius` (PRE 1737.400, i.e. **6949.6 km under**); extent ×**5.0000**; `inheritedScaling` tracks Moon `scaling` sample for sample; model `ecl` = **1737.400 km in every leg**. Screen: **+267 667 / −40 525 px** PRE→POST scaled; present-vs-absent, the rover draws **243 118 px** and casts **40 534 px** of shadow, where PRE it draws **0 px and still darkens 24 560 px** — a shadow from a body visible nowhere. **CONTROL: the unscaled scene differs by 0 brighter / 3 darker px of 4 194 304, and post-reload by 0/0.** Both reversible pairs traversed twice: four bit-identical exit screens (max abs diff **0**). **Physics unscaled VERIFIED, correction magnitude zero**: caster, receiver and light radii are all `getRadius()` already [`ModularBody.cpp:70`, `ModularSystem.cpp:417`, `:304`]; the ONE exception, `RingModule`'s `outerRadius * mc`, is scaled by the 2026-07-18 extent contract, points opposite to D21, and is **SUSPENDED for Vixy** (unexercised today: only Moon/Sun carry a scale and neither has rings). **§11.101(f)'s prediction REFUTED** — reload does not move the rover, because `reloadCurrentSystem` never re-applies ANY display scaling (Moon `scaling` 5 → 1 across a reload, both binaries) ⇒ **NEW §5.104**. **§5.103 CLOSED** (`JsonNum.hpp`, exact-for-finite by construction; `harness/dumpread.py` becomes the reader-side single authority, `b24_equivalence.sanitize_nonfinite` re-exported). Regression: A–D battery pre/post **0 non-numeric differences** over 5 scenes × 152 records; `b24_equivalence` 120/120; `b24_screen` all gates. **D11 cost ≤ 2.3 µs/frame (≤ 0.23 %)** — inside the instrument's own A/A2 floor, central +1.29 µs against ~1.2 µs predicted from the cause. Reach deliberately **bit-identical** (`updateReach` divides by the factor it now multiplies by; §11.149(c6) undecided, §11.96(e) suspended). `flag moon_scaled off` retired as a WORKAROUND, retained as a **scene declaration** in its seven harnesses, with the argument. Also **NEW §5.105** (the old path dumps uninitialized memory as `dist` for 8–9 never-sorted bodies) and two recorded observations: `moveto lon` vs `orbit_lon` differ by **60°** free / **90°** surface (F40's discriminator), and `ASmooth` settles a 5→1 ramp at **1.00000012**. […] → INTENT/11.152.md

153. **§5.80 CLOSED — the freeMode converter becomes the composer's EXACT inverse, so entering and leaving free flight moves the observer by nothing (2026-08-26, Opus 5 executor, task F40, dispatched by Claude Fable 5; code `7ef11aca → 18b6f13f`, `Camera.cpp`/`Camera.hpp` only).** Authorized by §11.151(a) [vixy]: *"Swapping between bodies must be transparent include the position channel"*. **The four** triple↔cartesian sites — `setFreeMode` both ways, `moveTo`'s free branch and **`getPlace()`, which §5.80/§11.144 did not count** — now read ONE authority, `posePart` / `posePartToPose` = `viewMat()`'s anchored branch solved for the eye and its exact inverse; `placeAt` reads it too instead of restating it. The fold cancels out of the requirement (`position == −pB` for every `S`), so the pair is fold-free and was licensed by `f40_probe.cpp` **84/84** at 8 poses × bound/unbound BEFORE the code moved. Live, one instrument scoring both conventions: **29/29 pre / 32/32 post**. Replacement criterion [vixy mandate + §11.19a; **sharpened §11.52(b)**]: every script-reachable seam in §9 routes new-path AND legacy commands produce visually identical output (A/B screenshot channel) — identity at the **reasonable-vision-capabilities** level, and **only where old was physically exact**: old defects that didn't reliably represent reality must NOT be reproduced and are tracked as defects (the named-divergence channel is that tracking). Then: `drawModularSystem` permanently new → old-path update off (A/B stays as harness mode) → ~~removal as separate Vixy-gated cleanup~~ **REVISED [vixy 2026-07-21, §11.51(f)]: both paths kept for backward compatibility *"in emergency"* through genuine public use — removal horizon extends past public burn-in.** Every "retires with the old path" item keeps its trigger (the trigger fires later, nothing reschedules silently), and keeping the old path building + A/B-testable is a standing capability, not scaffolding.

| A40 | **Teardown vs an incomplete frame — §5.59's fork** (NEW 2026-07-31, §11.126(g)(l)) | When the drawing worker has not completed the frame the main loop is waiting for and the user asks the application to quit, should it **(i) keep waiting** — today's behaviour, the process does not begin to exit at all and a 45 s watchdog calls it hung (measured 1/30 under load on the delivered binary, 3/30 pre-fix) — or **(ii) stop waiting**, which measurably reaches a teardown fault that waiting had been hiding (**3 of 3** cycles that entered that path died with SIGSEGV, across 3 arms / 60 loaded cycles)? Both are user-visible and bad in different ways, so it is a product-risk judgement, not an implementation choice. The mechanism for (ii) is written, measured and withdrawn; either answer is testable in three launches with `harness/f19_stall.sh`. **A third answer exists and needs Vixy too**: fix the fault (ii) reaches and then take (ii) — that is more work, not a different question. | §11.126, §5.59 |
| A41 | **The G4 EARLY-visibility gate: 2 px or 3.07 px?** (NEW 2026-07-31, §11.127(b), F18) | The constant that names this gate has always said **2 px** ("minimal size of the body on screen for showing the outer BodyModule"); the gate that has always SHIPPED is **3.07 px** at a 2048-wide render. F18 made the pixels the single authority and, under D8's as-if rule, kept the SHIPPED value — so nothing changed for anyone, and the disagreement is now visible instead of hidden in a fraction. Which is right is a product question about when a body stops being a dot and becomes a disc, and it moves every body's regime in every scene, so it is not an implementation choice. Reversing it is a one-token edit of `BODY_EARLY_VISIBILITY_BOUNDING_SIZE` plus the scene battery. Riding the same decision: `RAYMARCH_MIN_SCREEN_SIZE` (0.025 ≈ 51 px) is the one screenSize threshold still spelled as a fraction — deliberately left, since it is a capability gate rather than a G4 regime boundary, but the px argument applies to it identically. | §11.127, §5.54 |
| A42 | **The two paths swap texture level at different sizes — align, or leave?** (NEW 2026-07-31, §11.127(d), F18) | Old swaps to the full-resolution colour map at **180 px** diameter, the new path at **409.6 px** — so between those two sizes an A/B compares two DIFFERENT textures, which has already misled one measurement (F9's 1.34 ratio) and which §5.53(a) has now shown is a real colour change on the two bodies that have preview assets. **Recommendation: align to 180 px while both paths exist** (§11.52(b) parity). Left to Vixy because aligning means engaging the big texture EARLIER, i.e. more VRAM resident sooner — a D5/D6 residency question on the hardware floor and a D10 headroom question, not an implementation one. The two gates were deliberately kept as separate constants so this is a one-token edit that does not move the regime ladder. | §11.127, §5.53 |
| A43 | **The Sun and Moon preview textures do not match their full-resolution partners** (NEW 2026-07-31, §11.127(c), F18) | A body visibly changes colour when it crosses the big-texture gate, and the renderer is not at fault: the loader prefers an authored `<name>-preview.<ext>` over any downscale, and the two shipped previews are different pictures — `sun-preview.jpg` mean [254.0,246.2,223.5] against `sun.jpg` [250.7,195.9,94.5], `moon-preview.jpg` [184.1,183.3,181.5] against `moon.jpg` [150.5,147.0,146.1]. A body with no preview reads 1.000. Exactly two bodies are affected. This is a **data** correction and therefore Vixy's channel: the installed field is frozen and corrections propagate forward through `spacecrafter-data` (§2.0 D9). Recommendation: regenerate both previews from their partners, or re-author them to match. | §11.127, §5.53 |
| A44 | **The RING shadow caster is the one SCALED shadow radius, and D21 and the 2026-07-18 extent contract point opposite ways** (NEW 2026-08-26, §11.152(h), F39) | D21 [vixy 2026-08-22]: *"unscaled for physics (orbit, shadow received and casted)"*. Verified at source, three of the four radii already comply: the caster's is `body->getRadius()` [`ModularBody.cpp:70`], the receiver's `body->getRadius()` [`ModularSystem.cpp:417`], the light's `systemStar->getRadius()` [`ModularSystem.cpp:304`] — all the RAW member, so F39's correction magnitude there is exactly zero. The exception is `RingModule::getShadowCaster`, which returns `outerRadius * mc` with `mc = scaledRadius / bodyRadius` [`RingModule.cpp:197-210`, `:119`] — and it is scaled **by a recorded decision**: the 2026-07-18 extent contract, whose own comment says *"the unscaled form was a latent defect — under body scaling the drawn ring and its shadow diverged"*. So D21 makes it wrong and that contract makes it right, and reversing either re-opens the other; the question is which layer a ring's shadow belongs to (the drawn ring's silhouette, or the physical geometry the answer names). **Not settled by F39, and nothing was changed**: it is unexercised on shipped content — only `Moon` and `Sun` carry a scale by default and neither has rings, so `mc == 1` everywhere today — which is also why it can wait for an answer rather than a guess. | §11.152, §11.149(c4), shadow-paths.md |

### 13.D External inputs (companion files, single authority — 2026-07-20)

**[2026-07-22 repo split: this file and every companion file below now live together in the `claude/` nested repository (branch `CC-harness`, same host) — "repo root" in the paragraph below reads as pre-split history; layout + conventions in `README.md`. Companion-file refs to `src/experimentalModule/INTENT.md` were corrected to `INTENT.md` (same folder) the same day. A fourth companion, `DECISIONS_PENDING.md` (2026-07-21-wave residuals view, compiled 2026-07-22), follows the same authority rule: it owns no decision, this ledger wins on divergence.]**

Three files at repo root, outside this tree, so they're reachable without going through `experimentalModule`: **`USER_QUESTIONS.md`** — the rows that are genuinely user-observable behavior (not internal-architecture questions) translated to plain language, each tagged back to its `Axx`/`Bxx` ref for exact re-matching; answers resolve the corresponding row here, not a copy of it. **Coverage as of 2026-07-20 (expanded pass, addressed to a heavy early-release tester — planetarium/sky expertise assumed, implementation knowledge NOT assumed)**: 26 of the 29 §13.A rows — A1, A2, A3, A4, A5, A7, A8, A9, A10, A11, A12, A13, A14, A15, A17, A18, A19, A20, A23, A24, A25, A26, A27, A28, A29 — plus **B4**; A23's question narrowed to a completeness check the same day (row resolved [vixy 2026-07-20], but "do your scripts use the two-body patch, and does a per-body boolean reproduce them?" is answerable only by a script author and validates B10's scope) (CameraAnchors: which anchor kinds are actually used decides the port's design, and only a user can answer it). Deliberately excluded, and the exclusion is stated *inside* the companion file so the tester can challenge it: A6, A16, A21, A22 (no user-visible surface) and the coordinate/threading B-rows. Two factual corrections landed in the same pass — the first draft's A12 question described the copy-paste cluster as *orbital* data causing *positional* error (it is `rot_obliquity`/`rot_equator_ascending_node` axial-orientation data causing up-to-155° *orientation* error, 28 bodies, Iapetus the one observable case), and its A5 question described `apparent_magnitude`/`slope` as brightness parameters (they are H/G driving coma diameter + tail length via the comet-tail formula, and no shipped body sets them — so the decisive sub-question is whether the tester's *scripts* set them). Both were derived-doc drift of exactly the §5.2 class: a companion file is a cached conclusion and needs re-verification against source, not recall. **`FEATURE_REQUESTS.md`** — the landing place for requests originating outside this tracker; created empty (no backfill — nothing invented to fill it), log format self-documented in the file. Triage direction is one-way and explicit: a logged request that turns into real work gets a row in §13 (A or B) with a pointer back to its `FEATURE_REQUESTS.md` entry; the entry's `Status` is then updated to point here — it does not get silently duplicated into a second untracked TODO. **Round 1 answered 2026-07-21 (tester, relayed by Vixy) and propagated the same day — full record §11.48**: 13 rows closed (§13.C list), 11 narrowed in place, 1 new row (A30), 13 implementation rows opened (B11–B23), 2 entries logged in `FEATURE_REQUESTS.md`. Round-2 questions ~~live at the bottom of `USER_QUESTIONS.md`~~ **moved to the dedicated `USER_QUESTIONS_ROUND2.md` (2026-07-21, Vixy's direction)** — live re-asks R1, R3–R11 (R8/R10 reframed per §11.51's resolutions: R8 = future-preference input under the parity meta-rule, R10 = completeness check on B27's code-extracted list; R11 stakes-dropped, both channels build regardless per B17), plus a no-longer-questions section: R2 (resolved §11.49(a), outcome stated with an explicit veto window — the tester's original answer was ambiguous, so stating the adopted behavior back is the cheap verification) and R12 (withdrawn, informational, updated with §11.52(c)'s naming). **Round 2 answered 2026-07-22 (tester, relayed by Vixy) and propagated — full record §11.70**: A28/A17/A18/A4/A27 resolved; B4's design-deciding half answered; B10 confirmed (patch purpose = fly-into/transparent bodies) with one derivation OVERTURNED by R4 (stop-and-hold at the lowest altitude, not the inward asymptote); B17/B20/B27/B29/B31 informed; R2 confirms A9. Numbering stable, `(ref:)` keys unchanged; the old round-2 section in `USER_QUESTIONS.md` is now a pointer (single authority, no fork). The split by **who can answer** (tester-only = what shows/scripts actually do; Vixy = design authority) was round 2's correction of a round-1 defect — some round-1 questions went to the party who could not answer them. Round 1's own two failures are carried in §11.48(d) and constrain round 2: **name the observable, never the engine's internal vocabulary** (the `ring_shadow` failure — a document that declares "implementation knowledge not assumed" and then assumes it), and **give options labels distinct from case labels** (the A9 failure — an answer's ambiguity is the question's defect). The tester also asked two questions back; those are answered at the top of round 2 — a companion file that only asks is a one-way channel, which is a self-imposed blind spot on the one party holding the shipped-show evidence. Neither file is authoritative over anything in §13.A–13.C; on disagreement (a question answered here but not reflected in USER_QUESTIONS.md's traceability note, or vice versa) this ledger wins per I2, and the divergence itself is a staleness bug to fix, not a fork to maintain.
